GPU/ASIC volume, content value and price hikes drive 
PCB/CCL to another strong cycle in 2027/28 
We are positive on the PCB/CCL sector into 2027/28, with a strong upgrade cycle, and the 
whole industry will remain undersupplied because our checks indicate 1) continued AI device 
unit growth, supported by increasing CSP capex, 2) PCB design complexity will boost content 
value (increasing processing time and lowering yield rate), 3) CCL material upgrade to 
support higher data transmission and power supply, and 4) price hikes in the near-term 
because of the supply shortage and LTAs in the longer-term for a better demand outlook. 
What is not in the price yet?  
The sector has outperformed year-to-date, but we believe the PCB/CCL upgrade cycle will 
continue to surprise the market because 1) stronger AI device unit growth (especially Google 
TPU, of which we project 8.5/12m units in 2027/28), 2) significant content value growth via 
PCB design complexity (higher layer counts, advanced form factor like HDI/mSAP, longer 
processing time) and CCL material upgrades (M8/M9, PTFE), 3) wider AI end devices (GPU, 
ASIC, switch, CPU and LPU, for example) will all require high end PCB s to support  
performance and 4) better cash flow and profitability via prepayment and LTAs.  
The overall industry is under a severe supply shortage at this stage, especially high-end 
products, with the key bottleneck being in upstream material (CCL, glass fiber and copper foil) 
as well as PCB equipment, based on our supply chain checks. The longest lead-time for 
certain PCB equipment (like drilling machines) can be up to 36 months, suggesting strong 
surge in demand from the supply chain. We don’t see any sign of the tightness easing in the 
near term.  
AI server unit growth and spec upgrade in 2027/28   
We estimate GPU & ASIC volume will grow approximately 50% in CY2027 and CY2028, 
reaching a potential 34mn units in CY28, up from 15m units in CY26. The key driver is the 
expected unit surge in both CSPs’ ASICs as well as AI labs, that is, OpenAI and Anthropic. Their 
aggregate volume is forecast to rise from 5.5mn –6mn in CY26 to reach 12mn -13mn units in 
CY26 and further increase to 20mn in CY28, implying a quadrupling in a three-year period. 
Some of the notable drivers including the following: 
▶OpenAI’s direct procurement compute power (excluding CPS and neoclouds) is expected 
to reach 11Gw in CY28, up from 3.3GW in CY27 and a mere 1GB in CY26. This includes AVGO’s-
sponsored ASIC of 1.3GW (Jalapeno) and 5GW in CY27/28 respectively, while the remaining 
goes to AMD (8GW in the three-year period) and, to a lesser extent, NVDA; 
▶Similarly, Anthropic’s own compute capacity is likely to reach 13GW in CY28, up sharply 
from CY27’s 6GW. The majority of this comes from AVGO (TPU V8i and V8.5) while 5GW is 
supplied by AMD of  in two years; 
▶Meta is likely to build 3GW of its own compute on its ASIC (via AVGO) in CY27/28 using its 
MTIA400/450 ASIC. We note Meta’s previous ASIC program in 2025/26 has been scaled back 
and pushed out so this marks the second attempt at ramping up its own silicon solution.   
▶We estimate Google’s own compute TPU volume is expected to increase to 5.5mn units in 
CY28 from 4mn -4.5mn in CY27 and ~3.5mn in both CY25/26. While the unit growth appears to 
be modest relative to the AI labs, we note the compute capacity is likely to surge given the 
TDP for V8 and V9 is materially higher than those of V6/7.  
While NVDA’s GPU volume is likely to moderate and market share is expected to decline (as 
most of its customers are building their own ASIC and diversifying to other suppliers), it will 
remain the dominant supplier to most CSPs, neoclouds and enterprises. Moreover, NVDA, 
along with industry is migrating to a multi-rack or large pod structure to offer aggregated

AI server performance to jump significantly every generation 
As shown in the table below, we see huge upgrades in compute power and TDP every 
generation. PCBs and CCL will upgrade along with AI server specs to reduce data loss and 
improve signal performance. PCB design is getting more complex from 20L -30L to 50L+ in the 
future or the structure will likely be migrated from HLT to HDI. For CCL, we witness a clear 
trend of upgrade material from M7 to M8 now and will see more adoption of the M8.5/M9 
going forward, boosting the ASP (a doubling from M7 to M8; M8 to M9 also doubling).  
In the future, along with strong agentic AI demand, we believe AI server design will further 
advance exponentially in the next two to three years. TPU’s TDP is expected to grow 10x from 
v8 to v10 within two to three years to further drive-up PCB/CCL’s content value significantly. 
AMD will equip PCBs with 50L+ into the MI500 generation and Open AI’s aggressive hardware 
production pipeline will provide upside surprise to the market. We can expect PCB/CCL to 
upgrade accordingly. We will have a more comprehensive analysis in the next section.

Summary: PCB/CCL spec upgrade and key suppliers in AI solutions  
The table below shows the key players’ solution and their PCB/CCL specifications as well as 
major suppliers. For PCB, we see an increasing layer count from 20L–30L now to 50L+ in the 
coming years and moving from HLC to HDI for more complex design. Our supply chain check 
suggests AMD’s MI500 series as well as Nvidia’s LPU PCB will likely adopt 50L+ design. This 
represents a longer processing time with a lower yield rate, and design complexity will 
support further ASP hikes. For CCL, M8 is becoming a standard material for AI 
servers/networking and we are going to see more advanced materials into 2027/28 (M9/PTFE). 
CCL is the most critical component in PCB and every generation upgrade (M7 to M8, or M8 to 
M9) implies the ASP will nearly double.    
Nvidia: It started to adopt M8 material from the GB200 NVL72. Rubin is expected to bring 
another modest upgrade: 1) introducing mid-plane with 44L design and M8 (or M8.5) CCL 
material, 2) most of the rest PCB/CCL remain on M8 with higher layer counts. The company is 
evaluating PTFE material for its switch board in the next generation. Also, we think Nvidia’s 
LPU can generate incremental demand for PCB/CCL with its 52L design with M9/M8 materials. 
PCB suppliers for LPU will be VGT, WUS and TTM mainly, while, in our view, CCL suppliers are 
Shengyi (major) and EMC.  
Google: TPU is expected to see the biggest upside in the PCB/CCL addressable market in 
2027/28 thanks to both unit growth and content value upgrades. The next section will have a 
comprehensive analysis.  
Amazon: Trn 3’s air-cooled solution will remain at a 26L PCB design (with M8 material), while 
the liquid-cooled version will migrate to 40L. for Trn3 is solely EMC and TUC are the only CCL 
suppliers for the Trn 3. We expect to see a more significant upgrade for PCB/CCL in the next 
generation Trn 4 (higher layer counts, upgrade to M9?) 
AMD: AMD is another key growth driver for the sector, with meaningful volume into 2027/28. 
The MI450, with 48L/M8 design, will start to contribute from next year and we believe AMD’s 
MI500 series will likely adopt a 50L+ PCB design with M9 materials. This will represent a huge

Google’s TPU to undergo rapid spec upgrades in next 
several years; its PCB/CCL will benefit accordingly  
The figures below illustrate the rapid production transition and specificiation advancement 
of Google’s TPU roadmap in the next several years. We highlight the following considerations: 
▶ The current V8i variant, which is undergoing HVM (through 1H28), receives a modest 
upgrade vs TPU V7 in terms of design and specification. For the latter, there are two logic dies 
and four HBM stacks encompassing TDP of approximately 1,000w. In TPU V8i, the TPD rises 
by 30% with the addition of one logic tile and two HBM stacks.  
▶ We expect the next generation of TPU V9 to undergo a massive uplift in design complexity, 
TPD and compute capability. We estimate there are 12 logic dies (4x times more than V8i) and 
12 HBM stacks (double that of V8i) to be the first to useTSMC’s N2 process node.  Given the 
rapid rise in chiplet quantity, we estimate the reticle size of the design to be 9.5x reticle size. 
The compute capability is likely to surge accordingly with the TDP of both training and 
inference to reach 4,500w, more than triple compared to TPU V8i.  We expect TPU V9 to begin 
HBM in mid-2028 and run through 2029. 
▶ We think TPU V10 will experience the next wave of significant specification and 
performance uplift as the number of logic chiplets rises further to 30, 2.5x vs TPU V9 and they 
are all to be fabicated on TSMC’s N2P geometry as well as being encompassed with 3D 
packaging for the first time. In addition, the number of HBM stacks will rise by one-third to 16 
stacks. We estimate the reticle size is likely to increase to 15x, 50% larger than TPU V9 and 
critically the TDP to surge to 10,000w if not higher!. TPU V10 is expected to enter HBM in 2029.

Implications for PCB/CCL 
We think the TPU supply chain will enjoy the biggest growth in 2027/28 along with strong unit 
growth and content value upgrades among all the GPU/ASICs. Shipments are expected to 
reach 8.5m/12m in 2027/28 vs 3.6m in 2026. We view increasing compute power, higher HBM 
density, and a doubling of ICI bandwidth as pushing PCB designs toward significantly higher 
routing density, tighter L/S, more advanced CCL, smaller via structures, and multi-stage 
lamination to maintain signal integrity and shorten electrical paths.  
We observed that TPU’s PCB ASP has risen 2.5x from TPU V5 to V8, and 4x for the 400Gbps 
switch to 800Gbps. In addition, we estimate 2.6x growth in TPU unit demand from 2025E-27E 
and 3x expansion in the 400bps/800Gbps AI switch market by 2028 versus 2025, or 30% 
growth in total, making TAM growth exponential in these years. 
The table below illustrates the structure of Google TPU v8. One rack will contain eight 
compute trays, with each tray equipped with four TPUs, four NICs, four optical modules as 
well as two CPUs (Axiom). TOR (Top-Of-Rack)/OCS will connect those racks together to form 
a superpod (via the Virgo network) and the Juniper network to connect with other functions. 
The increasing compute power and connectivity represents significant content value growth 
for PCB/CCL based on those performance upgrades and data transmission.  
To support the performance upgrades, TPU PCB architecture is shifting from standard 
multilayer (Ghostfish) to N+M structures using dual -grade CCL and multi-lamination in 
Zebrafish and Sunfish, and ultimately to HDI PCB in Humufish. These transitions lift PCB ASPs 
by 30% in Ghostfish, 80%–90% in Sunfish/Zebrafish. Based on our estimates, content value 
per rack for PCB is up to USD30k-40k and CCL USD12-18k for TPU v8 (vs USD20k/7k in 
v7). We expect another 40%–50% in Humufish with HDI adoption. Into TPU V10, given 
that TDP will further double to 10kW, we can assume PCB/CCL will be further upgraded 
to meet the higher requirement.

Source: Aletheia/TAG 
We see an increasing trend for adopting HDI in advanced AI acclerators: 1) it shortens high-
frequency signal paths, reducing signal attenuation and latency, 2) it increases circuit density 
through microvia technology, including blind and buried vias, 3) it saves internal space and 
4) it optimizes heat dissipation efficiency. HDI normally requires fewer layers compared to 
conventinal multi-lam PCB due to its higher density, but HDI PCB is still more challenging 
because it requires multiple cycles of laser drilling, sequential buildup, and ultra-fine feature 
formation. The longer processing time and lower yield rate will further drive up content value 
for PCB.

Increasing networking growth will be another pillar of growth 
ToR (Top‑of‑Rack) switch demand for AI server systems is set to grow the fastest during 2026–
28 as architectures scale-up and bandwidth requirements rise. As scale ‑up bandwidth 
increases, data move outward more quickly, shifting bottlenecks in the scale‑out network. 
Google’s TPU v8 network architecture is a good example as illustrated below.  
FIGURE 12: MLCC DEMAND IN VARIOUS AI SERVERS AND GENERAL SERVERS 
Source: Aletheia/TAG 
Adoption of 800 Gbps switches is set to accelerate in 2026–27, with CCL moving from M7 to 
M8 and PCB architecture shifting from multilayer to N+M. As a result, we estimate the 800 
Gbps switch PCB ASP will rise ~275% to ~USD1,500 from USD350 –400 in 400 Gbps 
systems. The layer count will increase by 35%–40%, with the remaining value uplift driven 
by higher‑grade materials and more complex structures. For 1.6T switches, PCB ASP is

### Page 10

Advanced Materials - PCB & CCL 
Goldilocks

4 October 2026  10 
expected to reach USD3,000–4,000 as the layer count expands to 44–50L and CCL upgrades 
to M9 (potentially mixed with M8 in power layers). 
At the Hot Chips Conference, a premier annual semiconductor industry event, at Stanford 
University at the end of August, Google further disclosed the networking architecture for its 
upcoming TPU8t (Zebrafish – MediaTek) and TPU8i (Sunfish – Broadcom). In addition to 
upgrading its Jupiter network (north-south traffic) architecture, which connects storage, 
general servers, and its global WAN with higher bandwidth, lower latency, and fewer hops, 
Google also detailed its new Virgo network ( east-west traffic). Through the co-design of 
network architecture, optical switching technology, and the software orchestration layer, the 
Virgo network facilitates scaling the maximum TPU cluster size to 1m units (up from 100,000 
units in TPU v7 – Ironwood).  
This upgrade highly relies on networking hardware advancements, particularly in optical 
communications. Our deep-dive research into Google’s new TPU network architecture leads 
to the conclusion that demand will increase for ethernet switches and optical transceivers.

Who will benefit most in the Google TPU supply chain?  
ISU Petasys is best positioned to capture this growth given its high exposure to networking 
PCBs (50%–60% of sales) and its role as a key PCB supplier to Google (50%+ of sales). We also 
consider EMC another great proxy for the TPU supply chain, because  of 1) its leading position 
in high-end CCL, 2) its increasing market share in ASICs (Google, AWS) as well as AMD, and 3) 
its better margin/profitability along with favorable product mix and price hike s. TTM is 
another key supplier to Google TPU for N+M products, and we expect its business to 
accelerate from 2H26. If Google confirms LTAs with its PCB/CCL vendors, we think it will 
further drive-up the supply chain’s P/E with more stable backlogs and longer order visibility.  
Price hikes will continue; LTAs are ongoing  
Another key growth driver to lift PCB/CCL makers’ profitability is price hikes. We have 
witnessed several rounds of price hikes already this year and expect more. Besides, our 
channel check suggests that CSPs are working closely with PCB/CCL makers for LTAs to 
secure advanced capacity and smooth the production process. This section will discuss these 
two issues and implications for the supply chain.  
Price hikes will likely continue  
Our supply chain checks suggest that CCL makers are increasing prices by 50%-100% YTD to 
reflect 1) organic material upgrades under the supply constraint, 2) a shortage inf upstream 
materials supply and price hikes (resin, copper foil and glass cloth), and 3) longer and more 
complex laminate process for more advanced CCL. We see price hikes across the board from 
all production lines from M8 material to M4  because tier 1 makers are allocating more 
capacity for advanced materials (which requires a longer processing time resulting in lower 
production volume). As such, entry-level products will be excluded and will partially shift to 
second-tier vendors.  
Based on current visibility, we expect more waves of price hikes in CCL in 2H26 and 1H27. We 
think each round will be 10%-15%. We assume better profitability for CCL makers in future 
quarters owing to 1) higher UTR under a supply constraint, 2) continued price hikes and 3) 
favorable product mix along with product migration.  
Are LTAs becoming new norm for PCB/CCL?  
Given the tight supply conditions and strong PCB/CCL demand for the foreseeable future, we 
are also hearing that CSPs are aggressively approaching PCB and CCL makers for LTAs to 
secure capacity. Terms will vary from vendor to vendor. However, those discussions send a 
clear message that end users are securing high-end PCB/CCL capacity by prepayment and 
LTAs for the next few years, because the overall industry may continue to remain 
undersupplied. Also, we reckon prepayment 1) will ease supply chain pressure on capex/cash 
flow, 2) make the supply chain more willing to invest under longer order visibility and secured 
shipment volume, and 3) improve supply chain management with better profitability. Below 
are typical LTAs between memory suppliers and their key customers.

New trends/developments in PCB supply chain and 
implications  
Given the fast development of PCB design, there is lots of noise in the supply chain about the 
future PCB demand outlook. This section will discuss the latest developments in the PCB 
industry and their implications for the supply chain, including mSAP adoption in optical, pros 
and cons for PTFE material (hybrid/pure) as well as CPO’s impact on the CCL supply chain.    
mSAP is becoming a standard for optical beyond 1.6T  
mSAP (Modified Semi-Additive Process) is not a new technology. It is an advanced PCB 
manufacturing method that builds fine circuit traces by electroplating copper onto a thin 
layer rather than etching away thick copper. It allows PCB to further shrink its L/S to below 
30um (compared to 30-50um for traditional etching). As such, PCB  can support higher 
density to support advanced electronic devices with a smaller/complex form factor.  
FIGURE 16: MSAP PROCESS IS SUITABLE FOR PCB WITH L/S <30UM  
  Standard HDI process        
L/S > 30um

Filling laser Cu plating Dry film lamination Copper etching Dry film striping 
  mSAP       
L/S <30um

Dry film lamination Filling laser Cu plating Dry film striping Flash etching 
Source: Aletheia/TAG 
To meet the requirement for higher integration and miniaturization, optical modules are 
increasingly adopting mSAP to reduce product size and signal loss. Our supply chain check 
suggests that 1) we are seeing some 800G switch mSAP adoption, but it is more like an 
optional solution to ally with a specific form factor according to a vendor’s requirement, and 
2) mSAP is becoming a mandatory process from 1.6T given the L/S is expected to shrink to 
below 30um.

Implications for supply chain: The key bottleneck for mSAP is more in the 1) upstream 
materials from Japan vendors, including carrier copper foil from Mitsui Kinzoku and Nittobo’s 
low-CTE glass fabric and 2) selected equipment like laser drilling machines and laser direct 
imaging (LDI) machines. The production lead-time has increased to 10-12 months (vs the 
normal 4-6 months).  
For PCB supply, we believe SCC, ZDT and Unimicron will be the key beneficiaries  of mSAP. 
Compeq is also being qualfied for its mSAP solution for 800G/1.6T switches, based on our 
checks.   
FIGURE 18: MAJOR PLAYERS’ PROGRESS WITH MSAP   
SCC 
SCC is a leader in the mSAP process among Chinese PCB makers. The company 
recently announced a RMB4.37bn private placement to expand AI -related PCB 
capacity (including mSAP). 
ZDT/Avary 
ZDT/Avary is aiming to become the world's largest mSAP supplier by expanding 
capacity 5x in the coming years at all its major production sites (including China, 
Taiwan and Thailand).  
Unimicron 
Unimicron raised its 2026 capex from TWD25bn to TWD34bn in its latest earnings 
call. According to the company, 70% will be used in ABF substrate and 30% for HDI, 
mSAP and new production site in Thailand. 
Compeq 
Compeq has passed qualification for mSAP in high-end switch products and started 
to enter into mass production from 2H26. The company will continue to expand 
mSAP capacity in both its Taiwan and China (Chongqing) sites.  
 Source: Aletheia Capital, TAG 
Potential PTFE developments in the future 
Nvidia is testing hybrid material (PTFE + M9) in the Rubin Ultra generation for switch trays to 
meet up with higher requirements of data transmission and may consider pure PTFE material 
for the backplane in the next generation. CCL makers are testing and sampling several 
solutions at this stage, and we will have better visibility into 1H27.  
FIGURE 19: PCB’S MAJOR RESIN MATERIAL DK/DF COMPARISON: PTFE HAS BEST DK/DF

### Page 15

Advanced Materials - PCB & CCL 
Goldilocks

4 October 2026  15 
M9 materials based on PPO/HC resin are no longer sufficient to meet Nvidia’s performance 
requirements. PTFE has better/stable signal performance (low Dk/Df) and the current hybrid 
solution with no glass cloth can reduce the usage of Q-glass, which is under a supply 
constraint. However, the hybrid solution is difficult to process (laminate/drill), making its 
yield rate very low and higher manufacturing costs. Also, the new material will take longer 
time for qualification.  
Implication to supply chain: We don’t think PTFE will become mainstream material in AI 
servers in the near future considering its low yield rate and high cost. It is more likely to be 
adopted in areas which require extremely low Dk/Df like Nvidia is testing it for switch boards 
and potentially backplane.  
For PTFE CCL supply, we think Shengyi will be the first source given its track record with 
Nvidia. EMC/Taiflex camp will likely be the second source potentially (EMC supplies M9 PP 
and Taiflex supplies PTFE CCL). For PCB supply, we think the major suppliers will be Chinese-
based PCB vendors (SCC, Kinwong, WUS and VGT for example) and the yield rate is still low 
(below 30%).  
CPO may trigger CCL de-spec? 
CPO is an advanced heterogeneous integration of optics and silicon devices in a single form. 
It enables the integration of both PIC and EIC and packages them in a modular form factor 
along with switches or XPU (including ASIC) via substrate or interposer to support high-speed 
transmission and power saving. In traditional systems, electronic signals are converted to 
optical signals with signal loss and higher power consumption. CPO, however, places the 
optical engines closer to the switch ASIC or xPU, reducing the distance that signals need to 
travel and improving the overall performance. 
Most common data transmission involves using a transceiver module to input data into the 
switch ASIC for computing, which must pass through the PCB. For switches, the PCB distance 
between the transceiver module and the switch ASIC is approximately 11 to 12  inches or 
±300mm. This results in signal loss and reduces the switch ASIC’s signal integrity from 40 dBm 
to 20 dBm, implying a 50% signal loss. This is despite industry efforts to develop advanced 
low Df/Dk CCL to reduce signal loss and latency. As such, placing the optical engine next to 
the switch IC will efficiently resolve this problem as illustrated in figure below. 
FIGURE 20: CPO ADOPTION LIKELY TO SHRINK THE USAGE OF ADVANCED PCB/CCL

Source: Aletheia/TAG

### Page 16

Advanced Materials - PCB & CCL 
Goldilocks

4 October 2026  16 
Implication for the supply chain: The CPO’s purpose is to minimize the data transmission 
on the PCB, allowing high-speed data transmission to take place within the IC level. In this 
case, PCB in switch systems may no longer require the low-Df/Dk CCL material compared to 
its use of high-end low Df/Dk CCL (M6-M8) for 400Gbps and 800Gbps switch today. As such, 
some people may be concerned that the adoption of CPO will slow down the further 
migration of CCL.  
More impact on CCL side but manageable  
As discussed above, we believe the adoption of CPO will impact the CCL side more (material 
change) instead of the PCB side. However, we don’t think it will become an immediate threat 
and the overall impact should be manageable, because 1) The design of CPO is not finalized 
and still faces challenges in higher manufacutring costs, lower yield rate, and reliability issues, 
2) Nvidia is more aggressive in CPO solutions while other vendors (like Google) still stick with 
existing solutions, and 3) the overall CCL industry is undersupplied and material upgrades of 
more and more new applications are taking place in to fulfill future demand.  
Hence, our conclusion is 1) CPO penetration takes time and the initial impact will be more in 
the Nvidia supply chain instead of the entire AI supply chain for CCL makers, and 2) the overall 
trend for CCL material upgrades will remain strong as other non-CPO application demand 
remains strong and the overall industry is still undersupplied.

More to Come 
We transfer coverage and reiterate BUY for ISU and maintain a TP at KRW270,000 on 30X 
FY27/28E PER. We remain optimistic for its accelerated revenue and earnings growth into 
2028E, driven by increasing PCB design complexity and accelerating deployment of 
Google’s TPU and expanding demand in high ‑speed networking. We believe the 
exponential growth compounded by both unit growth and content value upgrades will 
continue to lead to the growth of its AI PCB business and offset the concerns about market 
share loss. Not to mention there will be more ASIC projects to come to further boost 
growth and diversify its concentration. We view ISU as the best proxy to capture solid 
growth from both Google TPU and networking upgrades thanks to its high exposure, as 
well as leading high‑end PCB technology and strategic geographic presence in Korea. 
TPU to lead growth, but more to come  
We expect ISU’s revenue/earnings to increase sequentially from 2H26 to the entire 2027 
along with the Google TPU v8 ramp up. Despite concerns about market share loss, we view 
that 1) the absolute shipment/revenue contribution from Google will still be more than 
double, considering the TAM is growing 4-5x with higher unit shipment (3.6m to 8.5/12.0m) 
and higher content value (80%-150% higher) for both of compute and networking, and 2) less 
involvement in Google’s general server business. The company is also working on customer 
diversification; MSFT’s ASIC will become ISU’s next key growth driver from 2028 onward.  
New capacity is on the way to support future growth  
ISU is expanding capacity aggressively to meet the strong demand: 1) an earlier Fab 6 ramp 
in 1Q27 for a 25% capacity increase, 2) A 20-25% capacity increase by 2Q28 after Fab 5 
expansion for HDI to secure annual sales capacity of W2tn, according to the company. Fab 7 
is also under discussion if HDI demand comes in stronger than expected. Its core MLB (multi-
lam board) products will remain in strong demand while new HDI products are under 
development. The company doesn’t believe the increasing adoption of HDI will cannibalize 
demand for MLB. Also, we see continued price hikes (along with upstream material shortage 
and a strong end demand outlook) and better product mix will drive ISU’s profitability to 
further expand in FY27/28 under a higher UTR.  
BUY with unchanged TP of KRW270,000 
ISU is uniquely positioned to capture two major trends due to its high exposure to Google 
and networking. We project its EPS will triple from FY26 to FY28 with larger revenue scale and 
a favorable product mix. We have a BUY and an unchanged TP of KRW270,000 on 30x FY27/28 
P/E. We roll over our valuation period to the average of 2027/28 with longer order visibility 
and more AI PCB projects to come, while we lower our multiple slightly from 35x to 30x to 
align with regional peers.

A Unique Proxy Standing for USA PCB 
We initiate coverage of TTM with a BUY and a TP of USD236 on a 27X FY27/28E average PER 
based on SOTP. We anticipate TTM earnings will double from FY26E to FY28E, driven by 
strong AI demand (N+M) and a solid footprint in aerospace & defense (A&D). TTM’s data 
center and networking segment will enjoy 79% CAGR sales in 2026-28 along with TPU v8 
ramping up. We believe its leading N+M position and franchise in the US will allow TTM to 
secure more AI server opportunities (like Meta) in the future . The company is also 
expanding its capacity aggressively in both Asia and the USA to capture more value in both 
AI and A&D space. 
Leading PCB maker in the US  
TTM is one of the world’s top 10 PCB makers and No.1 in the USA, as well as a total solutions 
provider for aerospace & defense (A&D) systems. We think its strong franchise in the USA and 
global footprint (capacity in North America, China and Southeast Asia) can help the company 
to seize opportunities in 1) content value upgrades in AI PCBs from Google and other ASICs, 
2) increasing spending in the A&D area and its tight relationship with the US government, and 
3) organic growth in medical and automotive spaces and continued M&A strategy.   
AI revenue to accelerate from 2H26 thanks to TPU, more projects to come  
The company reported 90% YoY growth of its data center and networking segment in 2Q26, 
and raised full-year guidance, suggesting this segment would double in 2026 with strong 
N+M demand. We project 2H26 AI-related growth will accelerate at 150%-200% YoY along 
with TPU ramping up to meet company guidance. As one of the major PCB suppliers to 
Google TPU, we see huge upside in this business: 1) TPU’s PCB ASP has lifted 2.5x from TPU 
V5 to V8 with more complex design and upgrades, and 2) we estimate TPU unit demand will 
grow from 3.6m in 2026 to 8.5/12.0m in 2027/28. Thanks to its strong capability in high-end 
PCBs, we believe TTM will penetrate more AI projects in the future (like Meta’s ASIC).  
EPS to double from FY26-FY28E; initiate with BUY and a USD236 TP  
We forecast TTM earnings will double from FY26E to FY28E with better product mix and 
increasing AI exposure. We expect increasing demand to accelerate capacity additions, 
supported by TTM’s leadership in high‑end PCB technology, its long‑term relationship with 
key customers and unique industry position (the largest US-based PCB maker). We initiate 
with a BUY and a target price of USD236 by using a 27x FY27/28 average PER. We use SOTP to 
derive our target multiple as we think it will better capture its dual growth drivers (AI and 
A&D), as its irreplaceability, in our view, make TTM the best proxy in the US to capture both 
AI server and national defence trends. Our 2026E EPS is largely inline with its guidance and 
consensus.

A Unique Proxy Standing for USA PCB

4 October 2026  22 
Largest PCB maker in the USA securing its industry position  
TTM is one of the world’s top 10 PCB makers and No.1 in the US, as well as a total solutions 
provider for aerospace & defense (A&D) systems. We think TTM’s strong franchise in the US 
and global footprint (capacity in North America, China and Southeast Asia) can help the 
company seize opportunities in 1) content value upgrades in AI PCB from Google and other 
ASICs, 2) increasing spending in the A&D area and its tight relationship with the US 
government, and 3) organic growth in medical and automotive spaces and continued M&A 
strategy.    
In 2025, 44% of company sales came from from A&D and 56% sales from commercials, which 
consists of data center and networking (31%), automotive (11%), and medical (14%). This 
year we started to see exponential growth from high-end AI PCB, thanks to a ramp up of N+M 
products for AI accelerators. We project data  center and networking will become the 
company’s biggest segment from this year, climbing to 49%/59%/62% of total sales in 2026-
28E.

Driver 1: advanced AI server PCB from ASIC/GPUs  
As one of the major PCB suppliers to Google TPU, we see huge upside for TTM in this business: 
1) TPU’s PCB ASP has increased 2.5x from TPU V5 to V8 with more complex design and 
upgrades, and 2) we estimate TPU unit demand will grow from 3.6m in 2026 to 8.5/12.0m in 
2027/28. Google’s TPU roadmap requires higher ICI bandwidth, HBM density, and compute 
power, driving a meaningful upgrade on PCB structure and ASP increases. We will elaborate 
more in the next section.

To support the performance upgrades, TPU PCB architecture is shifting from standard 
multilayer (Ghostfish) to N+M structures using dual -grade CCL and multi-lamination in 
Zebrafish and Sunfish, and ultimately to HDI PCB in Humufish. These transitions increase PCB 
ASPs by 30% in Ghostfish, 80%–90% in Sunfish/Zebrafish. Based on our estimates, content 
value per rack for PCBs is up to USD30k-40k and CCL USD12k-18k for TPU v8 (vs USD20k/7k 
in v7). We expect another 40%–50% in Humufish with HDI adoption. Into TPU V10, given that 
TDP will further double to 10kW, we can assume that PCB/CCL will be further upgraded to 
meet the higher requirement.

 Multi-layer Multi-layer N+M N+M HDI HDI

N+M sequential lamination is inherently more challenging than a standard 
multilayer build because it requires multiple lamination cycles, yield rates could 
see 15-30% loss 
HDI PCB requires less layers owing to its 
high density, but it is more challenging 
than N+M because it requires multiple 
cycles of laser drilling, sequential 
buildup and ultra fine feature formation. 
That would see further yield rate loss. 
ASP Index 1 1.3 2.2 2.1 2.5 ~5-6 
Source: Aletheia Capital, TAG 
We see an increasing trend for adopting HDI in advanced AI acclerators: 1) it shortens high-
frequency signal paths, reducing signal attenuation and latency, 2) it increases circuit density 
through microvia technology, including blind and buried vias, 3) it saves internal space and 
4) it optimizes heat dissipation efficiency. HDI normally requires fewer layers compared to 
conventional multi-lam PCB due to its higher density, but HDI PCB is still more challenging 
because it requires multiple cycles of laser drilling, sequential buildup, and ultra-fine feature 
formation. The longer processing and lower yield rate will further drive up content value for 
PCB.  
We believe its leading N+M position and franchise in the US allows TTM secure more AI server 
opportunities in the future. Apart from Google, we reckon TTM is also one of the key suppliers 
for Nvidia switch board PCBs and upcoming LPU, which can generate incremental demand 
for PCB/CCL with its 52L design with M9/M8 materials. Also, our supply chain suggests TTM is 
qualified to be one of the major PCB suppliers for Meta’s ASICs (MTIA series), which will have 
more meaningful volume in the coming years. 
Driver 2: A&D and medical segment growth from both organic and external  
Aerospace & Defense represented approximately 46% of 2025 revenue and has become one 
of TTM's most important structural growth platforms. According to the company, the current 
program backlog of A&D has reached an historical high level of USD1.7bn. TTM's A&D strategy 
is to move vertically through the electronics supply chain  from PCB/components to 
module/system solution providers as shown below. This vertical integration is strategically 
important because TTM can potentially increase its dollar content as customers move from 
individual components toward increasingly integrated electronic systems.  
FIGURE 7: TTM’S VERTICAL INTEGRATION INTO SYSTEM LEVEL

Source: Aletheia Capital, TAG 
TTM is investing heavily to expand North American capacity for A&D, including ultra HDI, 
munitions acceleration, defense growth, PCB reshoring and system design & integration.  
Given the sensitivity of the business, it is critical to build capacity in the US for core 
components. Management expects these investments to enable more than USD500mn of 
incremental projected throughput by 2028.

Apart from organic growth, the company is also using M&A to aggressive expand its A&D 
business. In August 2026, TTM announced it would acquire Epiq, a solution provider of RF 
products, space compute, and electronic warfare applications, for approximately USD$1.1bn 
(see the press release for more details). The acquisition will elevate TTM’s vertical integration 
capabilities for its integrated electronics products and expands its addressable opportunities 
in high-priority defense mission areas. According to the company, the acquisition is expected 
to be immediately accretive to EBITDA margins and accretive to non-GAAP diluted EPS during 
2028 for the A&D business.  
Earlier this year, TTM also announced two acquisitions: Swiss Technology Group (STG) & ILFA. 
Both are European PCB companies (see the press release for more details). We view it is a 
milestone for TTM in establishing its Europe footprint. STG is a Swiss-based PCB company 
focused on medical (surgical robotics, hearing aids, medical imaging and implantable 
solutions). Its focus on miniaturized and small form-factor technology is also highly relevant 
for TTM’s A&D business. ILFA has a 45-year legacy as a German provider of complex PCB 
solutions spanning A&D, industrial, and medical technology markets.

BUY: a unique proxy standing for USA PCB  
We view TTM as a unique proxy among all PCB peers given its presence in the US with leading 
advanced capacity and tight relationships with key customers. AI PCB will lead the growth at 
a 75% CAGR in 2026-28 and boost the group sales CAGR at 39%. We forecast TTM earnings will 
double from FY26E to FY28E with better product mix and increasing AI exposure. We estimate 
ROE will expand to 26%/32% in 2026/27. 
We expect increasing demand to accelerate capacity additions, supported by TTM’s 
leadership in high‑end PCB technology, its long‑term relationship with key customers and 
unique industry position (the largest US-based PCB maker). In the meantime, the continued 
investment in high-end PCB (ultra HDI) and system integration will further secure TTM ’s 
leading position in the A&D space as one of the few key suppliers. We initiate with a BUY and 
a target price of USD236 by using a 27x FY27/28 average PER.

Leader in CCL 
We transfer coverage and reiterate BUY and set a NT$10,000 target price for EMC, based 
on 30x FY27/28E P/E, supported by continued material upgrades and price hikes for high-
end CCL. EMC is the best proxy to seize strong upside from ASIC AI into production ramping 
up (exposure to both Google TPU and AWS Trn3) into 2H26/2027. We estimate TPU unit 
demand will grow from 3.6m in 2026 to 8.5/12.0m in 2027/2028. EMC’s capacity will double 
by the end of 2028 (vs ‘25) to support a strong demand pipeline and new AI CCL 
opportunities (more ASIC/GPU projects, HDI adoption and PTFE, for example) We think 
EMC’s leading position will be more secured when migrating to more advanced CCL 
materials with fewer qualified suppliers.  
Unstoppable upgrade trend in CCL  
We believe the CCL upgrade cycle will continue to surprise the market because 1) stronger AI 
device unit growth (especially Google TPU we project 8.5/12m units in 2027/28), 2) significant 
content value growth via CCL material upgrades (M8/M9, PTFE), 3) wider AI end devices (GPU, 
ASIC, switches, CPU and LPU, for example) will all require high end PCB/CCL to support 
performance and 4) better cash flow and profitability via prepayment and LTAs. The overall 
industry is under a severe supply shortage at this stage, especially high-end products, with 
the key bottleneck in upstream material (CCL, glass fibre and copper foil) to trigger further 
rounds of price hikes. We believe EMC’s leading position will become more solid when 
migrating to more advanced CCL materials (M8/M9) with 1) fewer and less qualified 
suppliers, and 2) additional capacity ready. 
Capacity to double by end of 2028 (vs 2025) to support strong growth  
EMC’s capacity will double by the end of 2028 (vs 2025) to support a strong demand pipeline 
and new AI CCL opportunities, including 1) further CCL upgrade s in TPU v9/Trn 4, 2) 
increasing adoption of HDI in AI accelerators to benefit EMC, 3) more ASIC/GPU projects like 
AMD/Meta, for example. M8 is becoming a standard material for AI servers/networking and 
we will see more advanced materials into 2027/28 (M9/PTFE). CCL is the most critical 
component in PCB and every generational upgrade implies the ASP will nearly double. 
Buy with new TP at NT$10,000 
We see a clear trend of CCL upgrades continuing into 2028 based on current design roadmaps 
and product pipelines. Its industry-leading position in high-end CCL and new capacity means 
EMC is the best proxy for upside from ASIC AI into production ramping up. We reiterate BUY 
and set a new TP at NT$10,000 with 30x FY27/28 PE. We roll over our valuation period to the 
average of 2027/28 with longer order visibility and more AI CCL upside for upgrades.

and GM likely improving sequentially in Q3, we believe Q3 EPS is tracking above
expectations.

ChipMOS Technologies 29 September 2026 ab 2
Forecast returns
Forecast price appreciation 58.9%
Forecast dividend yield 1.2%
Forecast stock return 60.0%
Market return assumption 6.3%
Forecast excess return 53.8%
Company Description
ChipMOS is a semiconductor back-end supplier with 45-50% of its sales from display driver
IC (~40% from TV and 40% from smartphone and IT and 20% from automotive), 35-40%
from memory (~40% DRAM, ~40% from specialty flash and ~20% from mainstream NAND)

and 10-15% from logic (TV SoC) and mixed signal IC (MEMS, PMIC).
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We have a Buy rating and a NT$166 price target, based on 23x average 2027-28E PE (vs. its
normalized 5-27x PE range). Our target multiple is in line with the average of memory and
driver IC OSAT peers.Downside risks include a weaker-than-expected tech end-market
demand recovery, growing China competition, failure to diversify into the SiPh/optical

component bumping business and a slower ramp of its logic testing business.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

### Page 3

Most of last week's economic indicator reports were strong: Initial jobless
claims fell to near a 57-year low, ADP private payrolls rose 90,000 in
September, and the ISM M-PMI was in expansion for a ninth straight
month. Friday's employment report was the exception. Nonfarm payrolls
rose just 29,000 in September, well below expectations, while July and
August were revised down by a combined 60,000. The unemployment
rate edged up to 4.2%.
Odds of a 25bps hike in the federal funds rate (FFR) at the October 27-28

FOMC meeting fell to 22%, with odds of one at the December meeting at
67%. Odds of two hikes by December is at just 3%. In other words, the
market still expects a rate hike this year, though it has all but ruled out
two. However, we still think the Fed ought to hike at least enough to
reverse last year's three 25bps "insurance" cuts. September's hike
reversed one, which leaves two more to go.
This week's data calendar is light, with Wednesday's FOMC minutes the

main event. Here's more:
(1) FOMC minutes. September's FOMC meeting minutes (Wed) should
reveal how much support the Committee has for tightening beyond what
the dot plot in the September meeting’s Summary of Economic
Projections implies.
The median projection of meeting participants showed the FFR at 4.1% at
the end of this year and next, i.e., one more 25bps hike in 2026 followed
by a hold lasting throughout 2027 (chart). Fed Chair Kevin Warsh said the

September hike "removed a dose of accommodation," implying that the
FFR remains below the neutral rate. The median longer-run neutral rate
estimate in September’s dot plot rose to 3.25%, a post-pandemic high.

### Page 4

The FFR futures market’s prediction is more hawkish than the Fed
officials’ median. FFR futures imply 3.3 rate hikes over the next 12 months
and 1.7 over the next six months (chart).
The 2-year Treasury yield is at 4.78%, well above the 4.00% top of the
FFR target range, with 12-month futures at 4.70% (chart).

### Page 5

(2) PMIs. S&P Global's flash NM-PMI jumped to 58.7 in September from
56.5 in August, suggesting that ISM's NM-PMI (Mon), which was 55.4 in
August, remained strong last month (chart). Services prices-paid
components deserve close attention, as the supercore PCED inflation rate
has been rising since October 2025.
(3) ADP employment & jobless claims. ADP's weekly employment
reading (Tue), covering the weeks ended September 12 and 19, follows a
rise in the four-week average to 20,000 for the week ended September 5,

up from a late-July bottom of 8,250 (chart). That pace closely matched
September's ADP private payrolls gain of 90,000.

### Page 6

Initial jobless claims (Thu) follow last week's 197,000, the lowest since
July 18. The four-week average fell to 200,000. Continuing claims
dropped to 1,701,000 for the week ended September 18, the lowest since
March 2023, with the four-week average at 1,739,000. The low level of
initial claims confirmed September's low unemployment rate of 4.2%
(chart).
(4) Global. Eurozone retail sales ex autos fell 0.6% m/m in July, following
June's record high (chart). August's data (Tue) should show whether that

was just a blip.

### Page 7

Bank of England Governor Andrew Bailey may signal whether rising
energy prices warrant a hike when he speaks at the Central Bank of
Turkey's conference (Thu), after three Monetary Policy Committee
members voted for one in September. The markets put the odds of a
November hike at 88%, with roughly 66bps of additional tightening priced
in for 2027.
Canada's employment report for September (Fri) follows August's 41,700
job loss, with the unemployment rate holding at 6.4% (chart).

元富證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市敦化南路二段 97 號 19 樓 (02)2325-3299  (1 15)金管投顧新字第 016 號 
公司拜訪快報

GIS-KY(6456) 
建議：維持中立 
結論：2026 年仍處於營運整理期

一、GIS-KY 觸控面板大廠，目前切入指紋辨識、近眼
光學業務 
GIS-KY 鴻海集團旗下觸控面板大廠，自 2011 年成立，
2012 年開始生產觸控模組 ， 成立之初是以貼合技術為核
心，結合專業的研發與製造，來提供客戶在人機介面的
整體解決方案。最大客戶為 Apple，近年除了將業務推
進至LCM 組裝外 ， 也積極發展指紋辨識 、 近眼光學業務。
2025 年主要產品營收占比，平板 61%、筆電 19%、指
紋辨識 15%、其他(車載+工控)5%。

二、2Q26 本業持續虧損，EPS-3.15 元 
GIS 2Q26 營收 105.02 億元，年減 44.8%，毛利率則從
前期 5%降至 3.3%，主要是因記憶體缺料使筆電及平板
無法拉貨導致營收大幅下滑，致使本業續虧。此外，因
母公司有資金與償還銀行借款需求，海外子公司盈餘匯
回使稅務費用提列，稅後淨損為 10.5 億元，EPS-3.15
元。 
三、未來業務主軸由人機介面轉向半導體光學 
展望下半年 ，GIS-KY 表示營收將呈現季季增的態勢 。 主
要業務觸控及顯示業務較為保守，指紋辨識業務穩定，
車載 、 光學及其他較樂觀 。 投顧預期3Q26 營收年減 17%，
主要是受記憶體短缺使終端客戶拉貨力道不足的影響，
估計 3Q26 稅後 EPS -0.1 元。整體而言，預期 2026 年
平板及 NB 業務佔比降至 70%左右，車載及指紋辨識則
約為 20%，光學及其他則有機會上升至 10%左右。 
GIS 表示未來策略重心將從傳統的人機介面，轉向「半
導體光學」(Semi-optics)，即利用半導體等級製程來製
作奈米級光學元件。其中，1)Micro-LED：用於 AR 眼鏡
的顯示，並具備短距光通訊潛力；2)SRG 光波導 
(Waveguide)：用於 AR，已在台中后里建置產線；3)車
用 AR-HUD：利用光波導技術縮小體積；4)光學鏡片：
Metalens、Pancake Lens 等，鏡頭用於高階 VR。光學
業務於 2026 年雖有專案開始量產，但營收貢獻仍小，
預計 AR 與 VR 相關應用要到 2027 年底至 2028 年，才
會開始有較顯著的營收貢獻。另外，光通訊業務主要聚
焦光源封裝，包括 CW Laser(連續波雷射)等產品，並同
步評估 Micro LED 於短距離光傳輸的應用可能性，公司
已接到美系 Coherent 的 CPO 光耦合元件 （800G/1.6T)
大單，主要應用於 AI 資料中心的高速數據傳輸，預料
光源封裝業務將在 26 年底有初步出貨，27 年上半年開
始陸續放量。為加速推動半導體光學轉型策略，GIS 今
年度資本支出預估約 80~90 億元。該筆資金將主要投
入光波導與微型發光二極體等新光學產品設備，其中有
約 40~50 億元主要用於光通訊雷射光源封裝 、 測試相關
設備。海外產能布局方面，去年 Q3 動工的越南新廠預
計於今年底完工，並力拚年底前通過客戶驗證進入量產
階段。 
四、維持中立理由 
投顧認為 GIS 目前仍以 NB 及平板觸控業務為主，隨記
憶體短缺使終端需求仍疲弱 ， 預期2026 年營收年減 15%，
營運表現未有提升，2027 年才會有明顯躍升的機會，預
期 2026 年 EPS -3.25 元，2027 年 EPS 3.28 元，維持
中立投資建議，目標價 80 元。

Document 268: GLOBAL MARKET CALL_ Bond Vigilantes Gone Wild

Source PDF: all report pdf/GLOBAL MARKET CALL_ Bond Vigilantes Gone Wild.pdf
Report source: 未公開來源
Date: 2026-10-04

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: GLOBAL MARKET CALL: Bond Vigilantes Gone Wild
Date:    10 hours ago
GLOBAL MARKET CALL: Bond Vigilantes Gone Wild

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
GLOBAL MARKET CALL: Bond
Vigilantes Gone Wild
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 4 Oct 2026
View in browser

### Page 2

Photo by Jené Stephaniuk / Unsplash
The Bond Vigilantes have gone wild worldwide, pushing government bond
yields higher in developed and emerging markets alike.
A month ago, we asked whether rising yields reflected stronger growth,
higher inflation, or looming fiscal crises. We still think the answer is mostly
growth. The exception is where government finances are weakest. There,
bond investors are charging a fiscal-risk premium. France may be on the

verge of a full-blown debt crisis.
Here's more:

### Page 3

I. Global Bond Yields
US Treasury yields rose on Friday even though September payrolls
increased only 29,000. The 10-year US Treasury yield ended the week at
5.28%, after hitting its highest level since 2002 midweek (chart). Higher
real yields account for nearly all of this year's increase, while inflation
expectations have barely budged. In the UK and Australia, 10-year yields
are higher still, at 5.37% and 5.36%. Japan's is 3.09% and still climbing.

The French government now pays more than Italy or Greece to borrow for
10 years (chart). The yield spread between the French and the German
10-year government bonds jumped on Thursday, when France unveiled a
2027 budget that freezes public-sector pay and most pensions to limit the
budget deficit to 5% of GDP. The spread is now the widest since the
Eurozone debt crisis of 2012. German yields fell last week as French
yields rose. Inflation is running hot in both countries, so the gap reflects

France's finances.

### Page 4

Six of the 22 bond markets on our list have seen 10-year yields climb
100bps or more this year (chart). France leads at 131bps, with the US
second at 112bps. Italy, Indonesia, Japan, and South Korea round out the
group.
Two-year government note yields are above central banks' official policy
rates in all six economies on our chart, by as much as 107bps in the UK
(chart). The gap is smallest in Australia, at 34bps. The Reserve Bank of
Australia raised its cash rate by 25bps to 4.60% on September 29, its

fourth hike this year.

### Page 5

II. Global Stocks
South Korea's ETF has nearly doubled this year, up 97.4% in dollars, with
Taiwan's up 83.1% (chart). The Emerging Markets ex-China ETF (EMXC)
is up 39.4%, and its lead over the broader Emerging Markets ETF (EEM)
widened to 15.7 percentage points last week from 14.9. We continue to
prefer EMXC over EEM. France's ETF is down 6.1%, the weakest
developed market on our list. The US ETF (SPY) is up 12.9%, in the top
half of the rankings.

### Page 6

Stay Home vs Go Global match has been in a stalemate in recent months.
The US ETF trails the All Country World ex-US ETF (ACWX) by only 0.3
points ytd, versus 1.0 a week earlier. The MSCI USA ETF (PBUS) and
ACWX have moved in lockstep, and both pulled back from record highs
last week (chart).
III. Currencies
The dollar has rebounded off the bottom of its long-term uptrend channel.
The US Dollar Index (DXY) is at 101.9, up from 99.2 at the start of

September (chart). We have expected the dollar to stay firm, and it has.
The euro's slide accounts for much of the dollar's gain, since the euro
carries a 57.6% weight in the DXY. The euro is trading at $1.13, its lowest

### Page 7

since May 2025 (chart). It fell sharply on Thursday, alongside French
bonds.
The yen is trading at 157.68 per dollar, near the middle of its 153-160
range since the joint US-Japan intervention in late July (chart). Japan's
latest data show no intervention since late August.
IV. Weekly Focus
(1) Seaborne crude oil shipments exiting the Gulf have rebounded to
roughly 16.5-17.5 million barrels per day (averaging around 98% of pre-
war levels, excluding Iran). Major producers like Saudi Arabia have driven

much of this rebound. While crude flows have recovered, refined fuels like
diesel face severe ongoing bottlenecks. Refined product flows remain a
fraction of pre-war norms (hovering well below historical averages),

### Page 8

keeping global diesel supplies and pricing tight. On Friday, the G7
announced the coordinated release of up to 100 million barrels of
emergency oil and diesel inventories.
(2) Inflation data across Europe underscored lingering stickiness, with
annual prints registering at 3.3% in Germany, 3.0% in France, 4.1% in
Italy, and 4.9% in Spain.
(3) In Asia, a hotter-than-expected Tokyo-area inflation report reinforced
expectations of further policy normalization by the Bank of Japan (BOJ).

Tokyo’s core CPI (excluding fresh food) jumped to 2.7% y/y in September,
accelerating sharply from 1.8% in August and pushing inflation back
above the BOJ’s 2.0% target for the first time in several months (chart).
The core-core measure climbed even higher, to 3.0% (up from 2.0% in
August). Services inflation also rose notably, to 2.3%.

Style Exposure
Fositek’s earnings upgrade cycle looks set to accelerate as Nvidia’s next-gen Rubin

Ultra raises both QD content and spec, with margin upside arriving earlier than the
market is pricing. We assume coverage of Fositek with an Overweight rating, and a
Dec-27 PT of NT$4,000, based on 22x 2028E P/E (implying ~ 60% potential
upside from the current share price). Our 2027/2028 EPS is 12%/40% above
BBGe consensus, reflecting three drivers: 1) near-term gross margin upside on
mix with stronger server QD revenue contribution; 2) QD TAM expansion into

higher-spec rack manifold QDs with continued share gains/new customer wins
from VR200 in 2H26; and 3) VR Ultra (VRU) spec upgrade where ZQD adoption
at ~2x ASP lifts QD content per VRU NVL72 rack ~100%. We believe market
conviction should increase on structural QD spec upgrades, share gains, and
another GM beat.  With this note, we transfer coverage of Fositek to Megan Hsueh.
• Nvidia VR Ultra partially upgrading to ZQD with ~100% content growth;

Fositek the major winner with new customer wins & TAM expansion to
higher-spec QDs. AI rack liquid-cooling content is moving from a volume
story to a spec and content growth story, and Fositek sits at the center of that
shift. Nvidia’s next-gen VRU platform likely upgrades portions of the system
from MQD/UQD to higher-spec ZQD, targeting better flow rate and lower
leakage, which raises both content and ASP. Our work implies total QD content
per VRU NVL72 rack rises ~100% vs VR200/GB300, as ZQD is adopted

across compute tray modules, tray entry points, and rack manifold sockets. The
key ‘why now’ comes from the 2H26 transition, where Fositek finally enters
into the higher-spec rack manifold QDs (UQD08 now) TAM beyond in-rack
QDs from VR200. In addition, the company is gaining share by winning new
cold plate customers (besides AVC) from VR200 as well, and continues to
sample to more, which could be future upside, in our view.
• 3Q26 preview: likely another quarter of solid GM beat, with better

product mix on significant QD shipment growth. Our  3Q26 GM estimate is
~37% (vs BBGe consensus at ~35%), implying further rapid growth from 34%
in 2Q26, driven by the better-than-expected product mix enhancement from:
1) the rapid growth in server revenue contribution (exceeding 80% in July +
Aug vs 55% in 2Q26) and 2) increasing shipments of higher-spec QDs (e.g.,
starting to ship UQD08 for rack manifolds from 2H26). Reported July and
August revenues each grew 13% MoM and 12% MoM, respectively, and we

believe the decent order momentum will continue into September with another
>10% MoM growth (based on JPMe) thanks to VR200 QD and initial new
smartphone hinge shipments, which should add up to ~NT$4.6bn (+23% QoQ;
+29% YoY), largely in line with current consensus.
• Understanding valuation as a QD spec upgrade winner.  Fositek is now
trading at 22x 12-month fwd P/E, in the middle of its historical valuation range
(15-30x). We apply a 22x 2028E P/E valuation to reflect a multi-quarter

Summary Investment Thesis and Valuation
Investment Thesis
We are OW on Fositek for its strong earnings growth potential
over the next few years, driven by rising server project wins with
an increasing customer base and structural margin expansion
with a favourable mix.
Valuation
Our Dec-27 PT of NT$4,000 is based on a 22x 2028E P/E, in the
middle of its historical valuation range of 15-30x since its OTC

listing in 2020, which we believe is warranted by its strong
earnings potential.

spec upgrade potential in Nvidia’s next-gen VR Ultra (VRU) system (upgrading to ZQD
with 2x ASP) from 2Q/2H27.

Key risks to our estimates
• Slower qualification in AI server rail kit customers/projects: Fositek has started
shipping its rail kit for some general server projects since last year and some air-
cooled AI servers rail kits since 3Q26. Also, it will start shipping some VR200 low-
U count rail kits in 2H26 for one CSP customer, and is qualifying a few more now. If
qualification takes longer than expected given the stronger-than-expected

competition from existing suppliers, it would be a risk to Fositek’s server rail kit
business outlook.
• Continued weakening foldable phone market demand: If foldable phone
penetration slows down amid the weakening smartphone market, this would still be
a downside for Fositek, as one of the leading hinge suppliers with still ~30%
revenue coming from it.

Financial Analysis
We forecast Fositek’s revenue to increase 47%/65%/37% YoY in 2026E/27E/28E,
supported by the company’s QD TAM expansion into higher-spec rack manifold QDs
with continued share gains/new customer wins from VR200 in 2H26, along with VR
Ultra spec upgrades (partially from MQD/UQD to ZQD with ~2x ASP growth).
Fositek’s 2Q26 gross margin expanded to 34% thanks to continued QD yield

enhancement. We believe the company’s margin will continue to surprise to the upside
in 2H26-2028, driven by the near-term favourable product mix from stronger server QD
growth and longer-term higher-spec product shipments, which should support our gross
margin forecasts of 35%/39%/42% in 2026E/27E/28E, respectively. The stronger GM
growth also serves as the key driver to our bottom-line EPS growth of 105%/89%/49%
in 2026E/27E/28E, respectively.

Investment Thesis, Valuation and Risks
Fositek (Overweight; Price Target: NT$4,000.00)
Investment Thesis
We are OW on Fositek for its strong earnings growth potential over the next few years,
driven by rising server project wins with an increasing customer base and structural margin
expansion with a favourable mix.
Valuation
Our Dec-27 PT of NT$4,000 is based on a 22x 2028E P/E, in the middle of its historical

valuation range of 15-30x since its OTC listing in 2020, which we believe is warranted by
its strong earnings potential.
Risks to Rating and Price Target
Key downside risks to our rating and price target include: 1) QD ASP/margin pressure or
market-share loss from competition, 2) slower qualification in AI server rail kit customers/
projects, and 3) continued weakening smartphone market demand.

### Page 1

M
Update
Makalot Industrial  | Asia Pacific
3Q26 Sales In-Line; Attractive
Entry Point; Keep OW
September sales reached NT$2.93bn (+24% YoY/−18% MoM), driven by shipment
growth of 22% YoY, ASP down 1%, and the balance from FX. Management noted
that shipments worth NT$80mn were delayed to next month due to volatile
shipping schedules.
3Q26 sales (+7% YoY) were broadly in line with both our estimates and consensus.
Shipments rose 1% (slightly below expectations due to shipment delays and order

cherry-picking), ASP was flat, and FX contributed +6%. Based on our simulations,
July and August GPM should exceed 3Q26 guidance of 24%.
Makalot kept 4Q26 guidance unchanged, with shipments expected to grow at a low-
single-digit YoY rate, ASP up 5–7% (supported by cherry-picking higher-ASP, higher-
profit orders), and GPM at 26% (-0.7ppt YoY).
We continue to expect Makalot's 2026 earnings to return close to peak levels. Our
net income forecast of NT$3.9bn is back to the 2023 level and only a low-single-

digit percentage below the record achieved in 2024. We believe macro uncertainties
are already priced in and view the current level as an attractive entry point, with
valuation at 11.3x our 2H26–1H27 EPS estimate and a 7.9% cash yield based on our
projected 2026 dividend.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+

2
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Makalot Industrial (1477.TW)
Base case value, derived by applying a multiple of 17x to our 2026 EPS estimate. Our target
multiple is below its average P/E ratio in the prior up-cycle of 21x (after re-rating in 2H23), and
is within the stock's 10-year historical 9-29x P/E range. We view this as reasonable, with 2026e
earnings growth of 8% seen outperforming apparel and footwear peers with flat to single-
digit growth.

Risks to Upside
n New non-US customer wins
n Faster-than-expected inventory digestion
n ASP and margin expansion via more complex garments
n Better US GDP growth
n Faster order reallocation owing to trade tension
Risks to Downside
n Deterioration in the macro environment
n Drastic appreciation of TWD
n Pricing competition initiated from competitors
n Tariff increases on apparel exports from Vietnam or Cambodia

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over
the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage

universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

4Q26 PCB 產業展望 
供需失衡、銅價飆漲，銅箔價格續揚

### Page 2

投資 顧問 
產業報告 
 2026/09/30

2 
4Q26 PCB 產業展望 
供需失衡、銅價飆漲，銅箔價格續揚 
結論與建議 
1. 銅箔過往主要以 HVLP1/2等級為主，而在更低的傳輸損耗/延遲要求下，進入 26年，多
數平台包括 AI伺服器及交換器，在銅箔的選用上已升級至 HVLP3，且部分終端已將規格
提升至 HVLP4，包含 NVIDIA Vera Rubin伺服器平台、AMD新平台 MI400系列；ASIC
方面，26年以 AWS T 3為大宗，以及 1.6T高速交換機同樣採用到 HVLP4銅箔進行搭配。 
2. 考量下一世代 AI 產品將逐步出貨，研究部預估整體 HVLP4 需求將在 4Q26 超過 1,000
噸/月(以 Hoz 計算)，儘管銅箔龍頭供應商三井持續宣布擴充產能，台廠金居也宣布增加
產能並持續將低階銅箔產能轉至 HVLP，再加上終端逐步認證中國供應商供給，但預期以
現階段的產能仍無法滿足未來幾年持續增加的需求。 
3. 考量 4Q26高階 HVLP產品需求大幅提升，銅箔廠商將持續將產能轉向高階需求，研究部
認為產品組合轉佳力度將由於銅價侵蝕之影響，整體來看，銅箔廠商將有望受惠於由銅價
走升所帶動之營收成長，以及產能逐步由低階轉向高階占比，帶動毛利率持續轉好之趨勢。

4Q26 投資展望 
1. 研究部認為銅箔產業漲價趨勢將持續，且漲價邏輯已由單純銅價成本轉嫁，逐步轉向高階
HVLP 產品供需緊俏所帶動的加工費提升。隨高階銅箔 HVLP3/4 需求自 2H26 因下一世
代 AI 產品需求增加下加速，而新增產能仍受到良率、認證及產能轉換限制，預期高階銅
箔價格將由成本轉嫁逐步轉向稀缺性定價，4Q26 供應商議價能力仍有進一步提升空間，
帶動營收與獲利表現上行，主要受惠台廠為金居(8358 TT)。 
2.  AI PCB受惠於 NV GPU AI Server 以及各大 CSP的 ASIC Server 升級迭代需求，同時也
一併帶動 CCL 材料規格朝向升級趨勢不變，新世代產品規格皆往高階原物料靠攏，且包
含玻纖布、銅箔在內等重要原料價格持續高漲，有益於 CCL 廠商持續執行漲價策略，故
維持看好 CCL廠商後市表現，包含台光電(2383 TT)、台燿(6274 TT)、聯茂(6213 TT)。

3 
高階 CCL 規格提升，銅箔供需失衡延續 
AI PCB受惠於 NV GPU AI Server 以及各大 CSP的 ASIC Server升級迭代需求，包括應用於 GPU、
ASIC AI Server 及 800G/1.6T的交換器板等高階 PCB需求，於近兩年產值大幅提升。同時也一併
帶動 CCL材料規格朝向升級趨勢不變，新世代產品規格皆往高階原物料靠攏。 
CCL 三大上游原材料為樹脂、銅箔、玻纖布。26 年初以來，我們看到玻纖布市場因主要廠商向高
規格產品靠攏降低產能下，產品價格飆漲趨勢至年底仍尚未停止，而高階產品也在需求大幅開出之
下亦將面臨短缺問題。而在銅箔方面，過往主要以 HVLP1/2等級為主，而在更低的傳輸損耗/延遲
要求下，進入 26 年，多數平台包括 AI 伺服器及交換器，在銅箔的選用上已升級至 HVLP3，並且
有部分終端已將規格提升至 HVLP4，帶動銅箔廠商營收增長，包含 NVIDIA Vera Rubin 伺服器平
台中 CCL需求主要 Compute Tray、Switch Tray、Midplane上，其中 CCL規格包括 M8~M9(含 
M8+)，AMD新平台 MI400系列也亦將採用 M8搭配 HVLP4等級規格。而 AI ASIC 平台方面，
美系 CSP 持續提高資本支出預算，同時自主研發 ASIC 晶片趨勢愈發激烈，26年主要以 AWS的 
Trainum 3為採用 HVLP4為大宗，而我們觀察到 Google TPU V8 則仍以 HVLP3配合 M8之 CCL
規格，預期將要於下一代 V9 才會升級至 HVLP4。另外，為追求極致的傳輸損耗，1.6T 高速交換
機同樣採用到 HVLP4銅箔進行搭配。 
圖一：各 HVLP 規格銅箔示意圖                                                                                  圖二：銅箔基板 CCL 
資料來源：三井金屬、國泰證期研究部整 
表一：各家 AI 晶片平台採用 HVLP4 方案規格

### Page 4

投資 顧問 
產業報告 
 2026/09/30

4 
過往材料主要供應商掌握在大部分日商手中，包含三井金屬、古河、福田以及歐洲盧森堡，然而多
數廠商在擴廠方面皆較為保守，導致高階原料出現供不應求。隨著更多的高階 AI 平台採用高階銅
箔，我們觀察到銅箔市場緊缺程度逐漸在擴大。因考量銅箔規格由 HVLP1/2提升到 HVLP3/4，產
能約略將下降一半，加上現階段 AI用高階銅箔皆以 Hoz為主，甚至未來採行至 Toz，將近一步降
低銅箔廠商有效產能。考量下一世代 AI產品將逐步出貨，研究部預估整體 HVLP4需求將在 4Q26
超過 1,000 噸/月(以 Hoz 計算)，儘管銅箔龍頭供應商三井持續宣布擴充產能，台廠金居也宣布增
加產能並持續將低階銅箔產能轉至 HVLP，再加上終端逐步認證中國供應商供給，但預期以現階段
的產能仍無法滿足未來幾年持續增加的需求。 
圖三：HVLP4 供需預期(以 Hoz 計算) 
資料來源：國泰證期研究部整 
銅現貨價持續上漲，有助銅箔廠持續轉嫁成本 
銅箔廠商購買銅線浸泡於硫酸溶液中做離子交換，再用滾筒製造銅箔。對銅箔廠而言，公司每月以
平均銅價加上加工費向下游 CCL 廠商進行報價，因此銅價格與其營收以及利潤結構息息相關。觀
察今年銅價整體維持高檔震盪並較去年明顯上行，LME銅價於年初 US$11,000/噸，一路升至目前
9M26 US$14,000/噸以上的水準，年內漲幅約接近 30%，主要受到礦端供給受限、冶煉原料偏緊，
以及資料中心、電網與能源轉型相關需求支撐。對銅箔廠而言，銅價上漲趨動產品 ASP 與帳面營
收成長，即使實際出貨量不變，營收金額仍可能隨原料銅價上升而增加。考量到銅礦供給持續受限，
以及美國恐對精煉銅進口加徵關稅之下，全球銅庫存持續下探，4Q26 銅價格亦有望持續向上攀升。 
另外，儘管銅箔廠商向下游報價採用前一個月之平均銅價格，在銅價快速走升的情況下，有時無法
及時跟進價格調整，導致毛利受到部分侵蝕，惟考量 4Q26高階 HVLP產品需求大幅提升，銅箔廠
商將持續將產能轉向高階需求，研究部認為產品組合轉佳力度將由於銅價侵蝕之影響，整體來看，
銅箔廠商將有望受惠於由銅價走升所帶動之營收成長，以及產能逐步由低階轉向高階占比，帶動毛
利率持續轉好之趨勢。

圖四：金居營收與銅價格走勢圖 
資料來源：Bloomberg、國泰證期研究部整理 
此外，研究部認為銅箔產業漲價趨勢持續升溫，且漲價邏輯已由單純銅價成本轉嫁，逐步轉向高階
HVLP 產品供需緊俏所帶動的加工費提升。 金居 1H26 以來於高階銅箔 加工費已陸續調漲約
5~10%；而日商三井金屬則延續 2H25 VSP漲價成果，並於 3M26啟動 MicroThin價格協商。隨
高階銅箔 HVLP3/4需求自 2H26因下一世代 AI產品需求增加下加速，而新增產能仍受到良率、認
證及產能轉換限制，預期高階銅箔價格將由成本轉嫁逐步轉向稀缺性定價，4Q26 供應商議價能力
仍有進一步提升空間，帶動營收與獲利表現上行。

李朋潤 / 網通、光通訊、光學 
+886-2-2326-9899#9814 / pengjunlee@cathayfut.com.tw

智邦 2345 TT  ( 電子–通信網路 )

不畏風雨、小蹲高躍 
投資與評價 
1. 智邦 2Q26 在 800G 產品強勁需求推動下，營運優於預期；因 AI 加速器與
高階網路交換器拉貨需求仍處產業旺季，3Q26 營運續創新高。 
2. 受惠資料中心高階產品規格升級趨勢，800G 網路交換器需求成長相隨，AI
應用增加美系客戶佈建更多高階運算加速器 ， 且網路交換器升級加快 ， 智邦
26 年 EPS 85.18 元與 27 年預估 EPS 107.53 元，營運續創歷史新高。 
3. 目前智邦股價 1,895.0 元，約為 17.6X 27(F)PER ，低於近三年參考本益比
區間 14~35X 中間偏下位置，考量資料中心需求快速成長與高階產品滲透
增加，26 年與 27 年營運可望接續創高，維持買進投資評等，由於營運旺
季將過，且受零組件漲價影響與 AI 加速卡產品營收快速增加，使 2H26 毛
利率略低於 1H26 水準，故將本益比評價略微調降至中間偏下約 22 倍位
置，目標價 2,380 元(22X 修正後 27(F)PER)。 
重點更新 
智邦 2Q26 進入產業旺季，營運為歷史單季新高 
智邦因進入傳統旺季與工作天數回升，資料中心客戶加速拉貨 800G 高階網路
交換器產品，再加上新一代 AI 加速卡產品也於季中正式量產，自 4 月與 5 月營
收皆創新高，6 月營收更是大幅躍升，2Q26 整體營收為 955.38 億元(+36.2% 
QoQ)，高於預估 7.1%，因高階網路交換器佔比自 1Q26 起已提高，毛利率
19.7%略高於上季， 由於新產品開發與產能擴充所需，營業費用 44.91 億元
(+23.0% QoQ)，略高於預估，營業淨利仍可季增 42.6%至 143.31 億元，優於
預期，稅後淨利 110.53 億元(+32.5% QoQ)，EPS 19.78 元，營運為歷史新高。 
預期智邦 3Q26 仍處產業旺季，營運將再創新高 
智邦 3Q26 仍處傳統旺季，美系資料中心客戶加速拉貨 800G 高階網路交換器
產品，新一代 AI 加速卡產品也於本季完整放量出貨，7 月營收雖與 6 月持平，
仍為歷史次高水準，8 月營收再創歷史新高，首次站上四百億元大關，預期美系
客戶對於 AI 加速卡需求依然強勁，故上修整體營收預估 61.4%至 1,219.21 億
元(+27.6% QoQ)。受產品組合轉變與部份零組件漲價影響，略微調低毛利率預
估至 18.1%，但也因營收規模擴張，使營業費用率下降至 3.9%，故調升營業淨
利預估 62.4%至 173.36 億元(+21.0% QoQ)，稅後淨利 133.65 億元(+20.9% 
QoQ)，EPS 23.91 元，營運再創新高。

### Page 2

訪談內容 
智邦 2Q26 進入產業旺季，營運創歷史單季新高 
智邦因進入傳統旺季與工作天數回升 ， 資料中心客戶加速拉貨800G 高階網路交換器產品 ， 再加
上新一代 AI 加速卡產品也於季中正式量產，自4 月營收 273.60 億元(+9.4% MoM)與 5 月營收
286.23 億元(+4.6% MoM)皆續創新高，6 月營收 395.55 億元(+38.2% MoM)更是大幅躍升、
維持成長趨勢不變，2Q26 整體營收為 955.38 億元(+36.2% QoQ)，高於預估之 892.36 億元
(+27.3% QoQ) 7.1%，因高階網路交換器佔比自 1Q26 起已提高，毛利率 19.7%略高於上季之
19.5%，但新產品開發與產能擴充所需，營業費用 44.91 億元(+23.0% QoQ)，使營業費用率為
4.7%，略高於預估之 4.5%，營業淨利 143.31 億元(+42.6% QoQ)，高於預估之 136.29 億元
(+35.6% QoQ) 5.1%，稅後淨利 110.53 億元(+32.5% QoQ)，EPS 19.78 元，營運為歷史新高。

預期智邦 3Q26 仍處產業旺季，營運續創新高 
智邦 3Q26 仍處傳統旺季，美系資料中心客戶加速拉貨 800G 高階網路交換器產品，新一代 AI
加速卡產品也於本季完整放量出貨，7 月營收 394.73 億元(-0.2% MoM)雖與 6 月持平，仍為歷
史次高水準，8 月營收 408.73 億元(+3.5%MoM、+59.4% YoY)，再創歷史新高，也為首次站
上四百億元大關，較去年同期大幅成長，預期在美系客戶對於 AI 加速卡需求相當強勁之下，9
月營收則續創新高，故上修整體營收預估 61.4%至 1,219.21 億元(+27.6% QoQ) 。雖受產品組
合改變與部份零組件漲價影響，略微調低毛利率預估至18.1%，但也因營收規模擴張，使營業費
用率由上季之 4.7%下降至 3.9%，調升營業淨利預估 62.4%至 173.36 億元(+21.0% QoQ)，稅
後淨利 133.65 億元(+20.9% QoQ)，EPS 23.91 元，營運續創歷史新高。

資料來源: 國泰證期研究部整理 
預期智邦 26 年因 AI 加速器與網路交換器持續升級，獲利創新高 
自 25 年初 CSP 業者加速佈建自家開發 ASIC 產品，並導入 AI 伺服器應用中，接續在年底推出
新版本，預期於2Q26 底放量出貨，日趨完整的白牌生態系可節省同等算力三成以上支出，可控
的產品發展進度降低了對於單一品牌業者的依賴。從ARM 架構 CPU、訓練用加速器、智慧網卡
與高階網路交換器共同構成美系客戶在雲端算力服務的高性價比競爭力，可推廣至更多中小企
業與價格敏感度高的潛在客戶 ， 期望在初期透過生態系的高黏著度 ， 使其成為該美系客戶的長期
合作夥伴。智邦因為美系客戶用於資料中心之 AI 運算加速卡與企業用高階網路交換器產品需求
強勁，受惠運算加速器逐代推出與美系客戶800G 網路交換器提前於 1H26 加速拉貨，使2Q26
定單需求強勁優於預期 ， 故上調智邦26 年整體營收預估 13.7%至 4,177.15 億元(+68.2% YoY)，
毛利率雖受 2H26 零組件大幅漲價影響與產品組合變化影響，略微調低毛利率預估至 18.8%，
仍較 25 年之 18.1%回升，營業淨利年增 88.3%至 604.98 億元，稅後淨利預估為 476.04 億元
(+80.7% YoY)，EPS 85.18 元，獲利為歷史新高。 
智邦 27 年受惠 AI 加速器與 800G 網路交換器需求成長，預期營運再創新高 
智邦 27 年因美系雲端業者持續擴大 AI 伺服器建置規模，又有新世代產品接替，預期於 2Q27
量產，同時 800G 網路交換器需求暢旺，再加上 2H27 可望小量產出 1.6T 產品，除此外，企業
客戶亦開始加速導入 800G，電信商需求也已持穩下，故上修整體營收預估 17.0%至 5,269.97
億元(+26.2% YoY)，即使毛利率較高之 800G 以上產品需求亦強勁，但因有 AI 加速器產品快速
增加，使整體毛利率將降至 18.4%，由於營收規模擴增，營業費用率也隨之下降至 4.0%，調高
營業淨利預估 9.4%至 758.57 億元(+25.4% YoY)，稅後淨利 600.96 億元(+26.2% YoY)，EPS 
107.53 元，再創新高。

### Page 4

AI 應用推動白牌生態系擴張，營運快速增長 
智邦 2Q26 在 800G 產品強勁需求推動下，營運優於預期；智邦因 AI 加速器與高階網路交換器
拉貨需求仍處產業旺季，3Q26 營運將創新高。受惠資料中心高階產品規格升級趨勢，800G 網
路交換器需求成長相隨，AI 應用增加美系客戶佈建更多高階運算加速器，且網路交換器升級加
快，智邦 26 年 EPS 85.18 元與 27 年預估 EPS 107.53 元，營運續創歷史新高。 
目前智邦股價 1,895.0 元，約為 17.6X 27(F)PER ，低於近三年參考本益比區間 14~35X 中間偏
下位置，考量資料中心需求快速成長與高階產品滲透增加，26 年與 27 年營運可望接續創高，
維持買進投資評等，由於營運旺季將過，且受零組件漲價影響與 AI 加速卡產品營收快速增加，
使 2H26 毛利率略低於 1H26 水準，故將本益比評價由中間偏上之30 倍略微調降至中間偏下約
22 倍位置 ， 目標價由3,000 元(30X 修正前 27(F)PER)調低至 2,380 元(22X 修正後 27(F)PER)。

公司簡介 
智邦科技股份有限公司(股票代碼 ：2345)成立於 1988 年 2 月 9 日 ， 主要從事企業級與電信級高
速乙太網路交換器、無線區域網路產品、寬頻產品、消費性電子產品製造業務，近年來積極產品
更新，推出 400G/800G/1.6T Data center 用交換器、Cloud Wi-Fi、Smart Home 等新產品。 
智邦早期經營模式主要替品牌廠商進行純硬體代工 ， 在由品牌廠商銷售給終端客戶 。 近年公司轉
向與軟體廠商進行合作或配合客戶本身設計的軟體 ， 可直接提供終端客戶軟硬體整合性產品 。 早
期做網卡、路由器、中小型伺服器起家，幫HP、Juniper、Huawei 代工，有優於同業之硬體設
計、測試與軟韌體設計能力，是取得資料中心客戶訂單之重要關鍵，一台 100G Switch 要 600
多個零件、測試複雜度高，交期、價格都須符合客戶需求，提高了產業競爭門檻。 
智邦 2Q26 產品營收分類為：Network Application 營收 441.10 億元、Switch 營收 495.39 億
元、Metro Access Switch 營收 9.42 億元、Wireless 營收 7.67 億元、其他服務 1.77 億元；
2Q26 以地區別分類營收為：北美815.58 億元、台灣22.92 億元、歐洲82.71 億元、亞太34.14
億元、其他國家低於百萬元。

智邦 ESG 政策 
1. 節能減碳：由於營運用電為公司排碳主要來源，故 2022 年起執行改善措施，包括細化盤
查，依據廠區使用目的，細化用電分區，設置分區電表，用電資訊上傳雲端，確實掌控用電
狀況及節電績效；生產優化，依據生產機台用電狀況，分析可能的異常，查出異常用電熱
點，予以改善；設備改善，廠區高低溫測試設備，評估測試方式，以減少升溫降溫的能耗。 
2. 董事會多元性方面：公司 2022 年設有八席董事（含獨立董事 5 人、約全體董事席次三分
之二，佔比約 62%） ，都是由各具備多元背景、充足專業知識、經驗與卓越見識並擁有高度
道德標準的業專人士所組成。獨立董事專長領域：2 位台灣籍（財會、營運、危管；行政、
營運、決策） 、1 位美籍（產業、營運、國際） 、1 位日籍（經營、領導、國際） 、1 位以色列
籍（產業、營運、國際） ，超越金管會3 席獨董之規定，確立公司治理專業化、公開化，以
全體股東權益為中心之目標原則，召開 6 次董事會，所有議案均向董事會提案後通過。 
3. ESG 創新：智邦企業永續委員會下設有環境委員會與綠色製造委員會，然建議揭露該部門
職責及是否取得任何綠色認證 。 此外 ， 智邦持續採取在地化採購策略 ， 且揭露在地化採購金
額比重，但建議公司進行綠色採購及發行綠色債券。建議公司強化此構面政策落實並揭露
量化成效，以利更加客觀評斷該公司此構面 ESG 表現。

### Page 9

投資評等定義

英文評等 中文評等 潛在報酬率 
STRONG BUY 強力買進 > +30% 
BUY 買進 介於+15% ~ +30% 
NEUTRAL 中立 介於-15% ~ +15% 
SELL 賣出 < -15% 
註:潛在報酬率=( 6 個月內目標價/本次報告評等最近一天收盤價 – 1 ) * 100

在進⼊今天的主題之前，想先跟⼤家分享⼀個好消息：我的⼆寶在這⼀週順利

出⽣了。

看著家裡迎來新成員，在感到開⼼的同時，我的內⼼其實也有⼀種很微妙的拉
扯。
這讓我想起前陣⼦在⼀次訪談中，我曾提到有時候真的很想給⾃⼰放個假、徹
底休息⼀下。但實際上，這對我來說⼀直是⼀件挺⽭盾的事。
⼀⽅⾯，⻑期追蹤市場，同時⾼強度輸出，確實會累積疲憊感，偶爾真的想完
全斷聯。
但另⼀⽅⾯，我⼜真的很喜歡看市場，⾮常享受去思考、去挖掘不同觀點
的過程。這對我來說不只是⼯作，更像是⼀種已經融⼊⽣活、戒不掉的習
慣。
現在的狀態也是這樣。我很享受家庭迎來新成員的喜悅，但只要⼀有空檔，⼿
還是會忍不住點開螢幕，看看這週市場⼜發⽣了什麼。
這⼤概就是我的⽇常吧。雖然有時候覺得累，但因為做的是⾃⼰真正熱愛的
事，所以過程依然很有動⼒。
所以，這期就不寫太⻑的前⾔了。這週我們同樣挑選了幾個值得關注的主題。

在⾹港跟⼤家⾯對⾯聊聊。

主題⼀  | Meta 的千億美元⼤棋： AI
代理⼈經濟
在發佈 Muse 過後， Meta ⽴刻再進⾏了兩個動作：⼀是宣布成⽴「 Meta 企業平
台」並從  MongoDB 挖⾛執⾏⻑；⼆是悄悄推出針對⼩企業的「 Muse for Small
Business 」。
如果把這兩件事拼湊起來，你會看到  Mark Zuckerberg 的商業算盤。
今天，我們從宏觀的終局野⼼，⼀路拆解到微觀的務實戰術，看懂  Meta 到底
想幹嘛。
1.1 | 挖⾓ MongoDB CEO ， Meta 準備進軍企業
市場？

### Page 4

Meta 從  MongoDB 挖⾛執⾏⻑  CJ Desai ，直接導致  MongoDB 股價⼤跌。
但我們先不談  MongoDB 的留⼈問題，來看看為何  Meta ⾮要進軍企業市場不
可。
千億美元  AI ⼯廠的變現焦慮
Meta 今年在  AI 資料中⼼砸下超過千億美元的資本⽀出。
過去，這筆投資靠提升廣告精準度就能回本。但現在投資規模翻倍，市場開始
要求這座  AI ⼯廠拿出第⼆套變現引擎。
Mark Zuckerberg 很清楚，單純出租  GPU 算⼒是⼀條擁擠且利潤微薄的紅海，
他想要販售的是「智慧」。
因此， Meta 選擇了⼀條層層疊加的價值鏈：從底層算⼒、模型、 API ，⼀路向
上封裝成  AI 助理與商業解決⽅案。
把技術包裝成企業願意買單的成果，才能攫取產業鏈中最⾼的利潤。
挖⾓  CJ Desai ：補⿑企業內部的「枯燥細節」
儘管  Meta 擁有頂尖的  AI 技術，但要將系統賣給花旗銀⾏或 Walmart ，⾯臨的
是截然不同的挑戰。
⼤型企業在採購技術時，評估的重點往往不是  AI 模型有多聰明，⽽是那些枯

燥卻致命的營運細節。
資料存在哪裡？存取權限怎麼控管？系統當機時責任歸屬為何？能不能跟現有
的  SAP 或  Salesforce 無縫串接？這些企業級實戰肌⾁，恰恰是  Meta 最缺乏的
⼀環。
Meta 最強⼤的是消費者脈絡，它知道你愛看什麼內容。
可是⼀旦  AI ⾛進企業內部系統， Meta 就成了完全不懂⾨道的局外⼈。
當企業  AI 要處理⼀筆訂單退款時，它必須知道客戶的購買紀錄、公司的退款
授權規定，以及內部財務系統該怎麼連動。
CJ Desai 橫跨  ServiceNow 、 Cloudflare 與  MongoDB 的經驗，完美涵蓋了資料
處理、⼯作流程串接與資安防護。
他的加⼊，表⾯看來是為了建⽴企業銷售團隊，真正⽬的是要幫  Meta 解決這
些讓  AI 無法落地企業內部的障礙，為未來的戰略鋪路。

### Page 5

真正的殺⼿鐧：讓個⼈  AI 助理⾛出聊天室
這條要鋪的路，通向的是  Meta 最核⼼的戰略企圖。
Meta ⼀直在強調個⼈  AI 助理  Muse 的「超級智慧」。假設你要  Muse 幫你買⼀
雙下個⽉跑半⾺的跑鞋，即使它清楚你的預算和鞋碼，它依然需要知道  Nike
有什麼新款、哪裡有庫存、能不能打折。
如果世界的另⼀端沒有系統能與  Muse 對接，它再聰明也無法真正幫你完成交
易。
為此， Meta 正在同步打造兩種  AI 助理。
需求端  (Muse) ： 掌握消費者的喜好。
供給端  ( 企業平台 ) ： 對接企業的產品、庫存與服務。
未來的購物場景，將由你的  Muse 直接跟  Nike 的企業  AI 助理溝通，⾃動完成
交易。過去  Meta 的模式是⽤廣告把消費者與企業湊在⼀起；未來，這個模式
會轉換為「個⼈助理、 Meta 平台、企業助理」三⽅直接連線。
Meta 真正的企圖，是將⾃⼰從單純連結⼈與⼈的社群網路，升級為連結個
⼈  AI 與企業  AI 的  「 AI 代理⼈經濟網路」。

然⽽，藍圖再宏⼤，也必須找到第⼀個落地的灘頭堡。  正⾯強攻微軟或
Salesforce 盤踞的⼤企業市場，壁壘太⾼、週期太⻑。
因此， Meta 選擇了⼀個更聰明、更務實的切⼊點：從⼩企業開始，悄悄接管他
們的「數位⼤腦」。
Amazon ， Microsoft ， Google ， Meta 分別在 AI 時代都有著不同的策
略，估值邏輯都不盡相同。⼤家要理解背後邏輯，才知道應該如何投
資。
歡迎你重溫第 60 期的深⼊分析：四⼤巨頭。
微軟失去 GPT 獨家反⽽更好？亞⾺遜估值
太貴？  ⾕歌需要 Gemini 變強嗎？
Meta PE 低是撿便宜？ - 深⼊分析第 60
期：四⼤巨頭

### Page 6

KP@FOMOSOC · AUG 19
在過去⼗年的雲端與移動互聯網時代，華爾街評估科技巨頭的⿈⾦法很簡單：
⾃由現⾦流收益率（ FCF Yield ）。
Read full story
1.2 | Meta 正在盯著⼩企業的「數位⼤腦」
Meta 在  9 ⽉底正式推出「 Muse for Small Business 」。
過去三⼗年，軟體公司提供的是⼯具。就像你買了⼀把強⼒電鑽或⼀台頂級咖
啡機，但動⼿操作的⼈依然是你⾃⼰。
因此，軟體業的賺錢邏輯很簡單，就是賣⼯具的使⽤權。他們按⼈頭收取⽉租
費，也就是我們熟悉的  SaaS 訂閱制，公司多⼀個⼈要⽤，就多收⼀份錢。

### Page 7

但  AI 代理的出現，徹底改變了這個規則。
你不再需要親⾃去學怎麼操作複雜的後台。現在，你變成了⽼闆，只要對  AI
下達指令，例如把新貨上架或是結算這個⽉的帳， AI 就會⾃動去操作底下的軟
體，把事情做完。
這時候，軟體公司的收費邏輯就跟著改變了。他們不再只是收取軟體⽉租費，
⽽是開始收取類似外包費或員⼯薪⽔的報酬。
因為你付錢買的本質上，不再是⼀個讓你操作的介⾯，⽽是⼀份已經被完
成的⼯作結果。
為什麼是⼩企業？
這場轉變會從⼩企業開打，原因很簡單。⼩企業是全世界最缺勞動⼒的地⽅。
⼤公司的運作邏輯是分⼯。財務部管帳、⾏銷部下廣告、客服部接電話。
但⼩企業⽼闆完全相反。想像⼀下巷⼝那間只有三個員⼯的義⼤利麵店，⽼闆
早上要回覆訂位、中午要跟供應商叫貨、下午要盯著  IG 貼⽂、晚上關店後還
得痛苦地對帳。
他們每天最痛苦的，往往不是某⼀件⼯作難如登天，⽽是必須在⼗幾件繁雜的
瑣事之間不斷切換。
以前的  AI 可以幫⽼闆寫⼀封⽂情並茂的催款信。但寫完之後呢？⽼闆還是得
⾃⼰去查誰沒付錢、打開信箱、貼上內容、輸⼊地址然後按發送。

現在的  AI 助理想要解決的，是這後續的⼀⼤串⿇煩事。它會先去會計系統找
出誰還沒付款，整理出⼀份名單，準備好信件草稿，最後只等⽼闆點個頭就能
⼀次發送。
這中間真正幫⽼闆省下來的，是整段找資料、切換不同軟體、核對資訊的寶貴
時間。 ⼩企業決策快，⽼闆今天試⽤覺得每週能省下五個⼩時，明天就會繼續
⽤。
這或許解釋了，為什麼  AI 助理的第⼀個⼤眾市場，極⼤機率是那些根本擠不
出預算多請⼀個真⼈員⼯的⼩公司。
你只需要學會「跟  AI 說話」
這次更新最⼤的亮點，在於那些能連上各⼤⽣財⼯具的連接器。

### Page 8

當  Muse 連上  Shopify 、 QuickBooks 、 Canva 以及  Meta ⾃家的廣告後台，它會
瞬間掌握你的庫存、財務細節與品牌語氣。
這代表  Meta 正在把原本散落在⼗幾個不同軟體裡的⼯作，全部集中到⼀個最
簡單的聊天視窗裡。
未來的⽼闆不需要打開⼗幾個不同的後台，也不需要去上課學怎麼操作那些複
雜的商業軟體。你只需要對著  Muse 下達指令，它就會⾃動去底下操控各個雲
端系統。
Muse 上線後，科技圈出現⼀種聲⾳，認為這只是在抄襲開源專案  OpenClaw 的
概念。 Meta ⾼層也⼤⽅承認，產品設計確實深受其啟發。
但事實上，⼩企業⽼闆最渴望的，向來是完全不需要學習成本的⼯具，⽽
⾮⽕⼒最強⼤的⿊科技。
更別忘了，臉書、 IG 和  WhatsApp 本來就是很多⼩企業每天聯絡客⼈、做⽣意
的命脈。
如果  Muse 能直接寄⽣在這些⼤家早就⽤習慣的⼯具上，它獲得的最⼤優勢絕
⾮技術領先，反⽽是無可匹敵的通路與普及率。
從「個⼈管家」到「數位店⻑」的⼊⼝卡位

看懂這次更新，就能看懂  Meta 對未來商業模式的底層佈局。AI 真正的威⼒，
在於它掌握了你們公司的內部商業脈絡。
對投資⼈來說，這裡有⼀個值得⻑期追蹤的關鍵問題：當軟體開始「賣勞動
⼒」時，整個產業鏈最肥的利潤會被誰拿⾛？是負責思考的「 AI ⼤腦」？是默
默記帳的「後勤系統」？還是每天跟⽼闆對話、直接指揮所有軟體的「數位店
⻑」？
上⼀個時代的答案是  Windows 和  Office ，因為誰掌握了⽼闆每天打開的那
個畫⾯，誰就能收⾛最厚的⼀層利潤。這⼀次， Meta 押注的正是那個「數
位店⻑」的位置。

結語：佈局未來的「 AI 代理⼈網路」
把 1.1 和  1.2 結合起來看， Meta 的戰略輪廓就⾮常清晰了。
今天， Meta 透過  Muse for Small Business 在⼩企業⽼闆⼼中培養出「對  AI 下
指令」的依賴，並成功整合了各種商業軟體；這不僅是在賺取「勞動⼒費」，
更是為了明天那個「個⼈  AI 與企業  AI 直接對話」的宏⼤⽣態系，提前佔據最
關鍵的節點⼊⼝。
Meta ⽬前還沒有真正建構出完整的  AI 助理經濟體系。它正積極補⿑這個體系
需要的兩端：⽤  Muse 抓住消費者，⽤企業平台（從⼩企業開始）拉攏企業。
接下來最值得關注的，就是  Meta 能不能成功蓋出中間那座橋樑，讓這個全新
的「 AI 代理⼈經濟網路」正式運轉起來。
當 AI 代理⼈出現後，電商的版圖勢必翻天覆地。
作為電商最底層的基礎層， Shopify 會是迎來更多機會，還是有被繞過
的危機？
歡迎⼤家重溫最新⼀期深⼊分析。
當  Amazon 關上⾨， Shopify 攜⼿

Meta ， AI 代勞下單的年代，賣「鏟⼦」
的還值錢嗎？  - 深⼊分析第 66 期：

### Page 10

2026 年  9 ⽉  20 ⽇晚上，有⼈試著使⽤  Meta 剛推出的  AI 助理「 Muse 」，到
Amazon 網站上進⾏採購。
Read full story
主題⼆  | 為甚麼  AMD 要花  82 億美
元買⼀家營收不多的公司？
這週  AMD 宣布，以全股票⽅式收購李⾶⾶創辦的  AI 實驗室  World Labs ，交易
⾦額約  82 億美元。
這家實驗室成⽴才兩年多，員⼯⼤約  70 ⼈，第⼀個產品今年才剛推出，甚⾄
連營收都還沒公開。

### Page 11

花  82 億美元買⼀家營收都不公開的公司， AMD 到底看上它什麼？
World Labs 是甚麼公司
現在的  ChatGPT 這類  AI ，很像⼀個讀過⼀萬本⾷譜、卻從來沒進過廚房的天
才。
它知道熱鍋這兩個字怎麼⽤，但不知道⼿伸過去會被燙傷。因為它只活在⽂字
裡，從來沒有在真實世界活過。
World Labs 要補的就是這⼀塊，他們做的技術叫做空間智慧。
他們模型的⽬的是：只要給它⼀張照⽚或⼀段⽂字，就能直接⽣成⼀個你可以
⾛進去、能互動的  3D 虛擬世界。
他們的第⼀款產品  Marble ，就是讓任何⼈都能⽤照⽚或⽂字，秒造出⼀個  3D
場景。
這項技術是未來機器⼈和⾃駕⾞的基本功。機器⼈在現實世界動⼿之前，必須
先在虛擬世界裡跌倒⼀萬次。因為在虛擬世界跌倒，才不⽤付醫藥費。
不過，這筆交易真正的核⼼資產，其實不是他們的產品，⽽是李⾶⾶這個⼈，
以及她⼿下的頂尖研究團隊。
⼗幾年前，李⾶⾶建⽴了巨⼤的  ImageNet 資料庫，⼀張張標註這是貓、這是
⾞，⼿把⼿教電腦看懂圖⽚。

後來的深度學習⾰命，很⼤程度就是從這個資料庫起跑的，業界因此稱她
為  AI 教⺟。
收購完成後，李⾶⾶會全職加⼊  AMD 擔任執⾏副總裁兼⾸席科學家，直接向
CEO 蘇姿丰報告。團隊裡的頂尖研究員，也全數打包加⼊  AMD 。
晶⽚公司的時間競賽
要理解  AMD 為何要買，得先明⽩晶⽚公司⾯對的現實。
⼀顆晶⽚從設計到⼤量出貨，需要  3 到  5 年。⼯程師今天畫下的電路圖，是
2029 、 2030 年才會裝進資料中⼼的東⻄。
所以，晶⽚公司的每⼀代產品，其實都在賭幾年後的  AI ⻑什麼樣⼦：

### Page 12

模型會變多⼤？吃記憶體還是吃算⼒？主⼒是語⾔模型還是空間模型？
賭對了，晶⽚⼀出來就是為當紅模型量⾝打造；賭錯⼀代，就是花幾⼗億美元
做出⼀顆為昨天的  AI 設計的晶⽚。
蘇姿丰在訪問中說到：「端到端懂了，系統才做得好。」
在「物理  AI 」的規格還沒被統⼀前， AMD 買下  World Labs ，就是為了買
下未來路線圖的定義權。
當然，隔壁的  NVIDIA 早就在跑  Omniverse 和  Isaac 了。 AMD 這次卡位不是⾛
進無⼈區，⽽是趁遊戲規則還沒寫死前擠上牌桌，補⿑⾃⼰的全棧能⼒。
82 億美元，其實⼀⽑現⾦都沒花
這是⼀筆全股票交易。 AMD 不⽤動⽤帳上的現⾦，⽽是直接發⾏新股票給
World Labs 的股東。
這細節為什麼重要？因為  AMD 的股票今年特別貴。股價從年初的  214 美元⼀
路漲到公告前的  607 美元，翻了數倍，公司總市值已經逼近⼀兆美元。
82 億美元聽起來很⼤，但其實不到  AMD 總市值的  1% 。
打個⽐⽅：這就像你⼿上⼀棟價值⼀億元的房⼦，你只拿出不到⼀百萬的房產

權狀，就把隔壁⼀整間頂級⼯作室給換了下來。
股票貴的時候⽤股票買東⻄，是最划算的購物⽅式，你付出去的，其實是
市場對你的信⼼。
⽽且這場聯姻醞釀很久了。蘇姿丰個⼈很早就是  World Labs 的投資⼈， AMD
公司也在今年  2 ⽉參與了他們  10 億美元的募資。
對  AMD 來說，這不會傷筋動⾻。發⾏不到  1% 的新股，完全不影響近期的獲
利模型，華爾街連⽬標價都不⽤改。
最簡潔的理解⽅式是：這是⼀張⽤股票付費的⻑期選擇權。
賭贏了， AMD 在物理  AI 的⼯作負載上有了發⾔權，軟體⽣態（ ROCm ）也有
機會縮⼩與對⼿的差距；賭輸了，損益表損失也有限。
主要的代價，就是⽤⾼價股票換了⼀個還沒變現的研究⽅向。

### Page 13

但真正的短板，從來不是研究
AMD 和  NVIDIA 的晶⽚，如果單看硬體規格，常常打得有來有回。兩者真正
的差距在於軟體⽣態，也就是  NVIDIA 護城河極深的  CUDA 。
CUDA 是  NVIDIA 花了近⼆⼗年養起來的⽣態系，全世界的  AI ⼯程師幾乎都
是⽤它學會寫程式的。
這就像⼤家都習慣了  Windows ，你做出再好的電腦，如果上⾯的軟體都要
重寫，⼤家還是懶得換系統。
World Labs 帶來的是研究⽕⼒，⽽不是開發者⽣態。研究成果要變成晶⽚設
計、再變成客戶⼿上的產品，週期都是以年來計算的。
World Labs 可以當內部的試⽤員，幫  AMD 找瓶頸、定規格，但從內部跑模型
到外部開發者願意使⽤你的平台，中間還有漫⻑的路要⾛。

三年後才能結算的⼀筆帳
這筆交易的成敗，不在公告的今天，⽽取決於三年後的兩個問題：
1. 李⾶⾶的團隊還在不在？
2. AMD 的軟體⼯具，有沒有因為他們變得更好⽤？
研究型實驗室的核⼼資產就是⼈，但⼈是會⾛的。
全股票收購，等於讓  World Labs 團隊⼀夜之間拿到⼤筆可變現的股票。科技圈
被⼤公司收購的明星實驗室，常⾒的劇本都是兩三年後核⼼成員陸續離職再去
創業。
買下頂尖⼤腦只是第⼀步。如何讓⼤腦留下來，並真正改變  AMD 的產品
路線，才是那  82 億美元真正買的東⻄。
值不值得，三年後⾒分曉。

Elon Musk 多次公開表⽰， Optimus 不只是  Tesla 有史以來最重要的產品，更可
能是「史上最⼤的產品」，他甚⾄判斷未來  Tesla ⼋成的價值會來⾃機器⼈，⽽
⾮汽⾞。
